# Quest Counter

Source page: [quest-counter.html](quest-counter.html)

Short jobs from the **Quest Counter** in each town's cafe.

## How it works

- Talk to the Quest Counter in a town's cafe and pick a quest from the list.
- Taking a quest costs **quest points**. Each quest list below shows the cost of every quest.
- You hold up to **15** quest points. New points past 15 are lost.
- Quests pay EXP, Job EXP and gold, and some also give an item.
- EP only counts for [DEM](races.md#dem) characters, who use it to raise their Cost Limit and level.
- Some quests have a **time limit**. If you fail a quest or run out of time, you lose the quest points you paid.
- Every quest you finish counts toward your **quest total**. Some places only let you in after you have finished enough quests, for example the [Ancient Ruins](dungeons.md) need **10**.

## Hunt Quests

Defeat a number of monsters.

Most hunt quests pay **EXP, Job EXP and gold**. Some also give a reward item.

## Collect Quests

Bring a number of items.

Most collect quests pay **EXP, Job EXP and gold**. Some also give a reward item.

- The Cafe's collect quests come from the **Cafe Master** in the [Cafe](maps.html#map:10024000), [Downtown](maps.html#map:10024000). Choose **Quest Counter**.
   **Cafe Master**
- These counters give the same quests:
   - **Valeria's Brother** in the [Northern Cafe](maps.html#map:10065000), [Northern Promenade](maps.html#map:10065000)
   - **Cafe Employee** in the [Iron City Cafe](maps.html#map:10063100), [Upper Iron City](maps.html#map:10063100)
   - **Cafe Employee** in the [Iron City Cafe](maps.html#map:10066000), [Lower Part of Iron City](maps.html#map:10066000)
   - **Cafe Master** in the [Fareast Cafe](maps.html#map:10057000), [Fareast City](maps.html#map:10057000)
   - **Cafe Master** in the [Fortress Cafe](maps.html#map:10054001), [Enigma Group Fortress](maps.html#map:10054001)
   - **Cafe Master** in the [Morg Cafe](maps.html#map:10060000), [Morg City](maps.html#map:10060000)
   - **Cafe Master** in the [Morg Pet Cafe](maps.html#map:10060000), [Morg City](maps.html#map:10060000)
   - **Cafe Master** in the [Tonka Cafe](maps.html#map:10062000), [Tonka Island](maps.html#map:10062000)
   - [Cafe Employee](maps.html#map:10025000) in [Acronia Eastern Plains](maps.html#map:10025000)
   - [Cafe Employee](maps.html#map:10022000) in [Acronia Western Plains](maps.html#map:10022000)
   - [Cafe Employee](maps.html#map:10031000) in [Acronia Southern Plains](maps.html#map:10031000)
   - [Cafe Employee](maps.html#map:10014000) in [Acronia Northern Plains](maps.html#map:10014000)
   - [Carrier Mermaid](maps.html#map:11053000) in [Water Layer](maps.html#map:11053000)
- To hand in, talk to the counter again and put the items in the trade window. You can hand them in over several visits. Extra items come back to you.

## EP Quests

Quests only the [DEM](races.md#dem) race can take, at any Quest Counter.

Every EP quest pays EXP, Job EXP, gold and **15–30 EP**. Delivery quests also give an item.

- Hunt and delivery quests that start below level 59 give **15 EP**. Quests that start at level 59 or higher give **30 EP**.
- Ecosystem research gives **24 EP**.
- The two party hunts can be done together with a party.
- EP boss quests at the gate open a private dungeon straight into the boss room. They have 60 minutes and give **30 EP**.

What each quest pays.
